title:The Best Auto Rifles
@description:Additional weapon rolls to those discussed in @PodvsEnemies Podcast episode 175. Destiny Data Compendium, Aegis's Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​ and other resources, available at https://www.destiny2.science/, or through https://www.youtube.com/@TheAegisRelic. The effect of weapon perks on stats can be found at https://destiny.report or https://d2foundry.gg.

// Snipehunt Mk.47 - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Light Touch), (Voltshot)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God Arc-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Arc builds. Snipehunt Mk.47 is a lightweight auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is left of vertical and very loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For Arc builds, in the first trait perk column, to speed up reloads, alongside the Tex Balanced Stock origin trait, Light Touch boosts Handling and Reload Speed, and provides an ADS scalar while you're Amplified or Radiant, or Devour is active. Then, in the second column, Voltshot applies Arc Jolt to the next target you hit within 8s, after a reload within 5.3s of a kill, for additional damage to the target on further hits plus AoE chain lightning damage on nearby enemies. Aegis ranks the Tex Balanced Stock origin trait as B-Tier for PvE - this procs a refreshable, mainly PvP focused buff after hitting 4 hip-fire shots within 4s of each other, which boosts Range, Handling and Reload Speed by +20 with a ×0.9 animation scalar, +1.5° Hip-fire precision angle threshold, better ADS speed, but also for PvE grants +35% stagger force. There's also the E-Tier Featherweight which stacks up to ×10 to improve movement speed. If you're interested, the name references a practical joke where beginners are led into the wilderness on a fabricated mission to hunt a non-existent bird. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame Arc-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Gnawing Hunger - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Withering Gaze), (Target Lock, Destabilizing Rounds)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-Champion PvE-EndGame PvE-God Void-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for piercing Barrier Chmpions or clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly for Void builds. Gnawing Hunger is an adaptive auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "probably best overall auto archetype accounting for barrier intrinsic and damage profile". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just left vertical but loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. To help defeat Barrier Champions, you could consider the combination of Withering Gaze in the first trait perk column to apply the Void Weaken debuff to targets after ADS-ing for 1.3s for ×1.15 damage for a refreshable 6s for your fireteam. If the stronger ×1.3 weaken has been applied from Tractor Cannon or Shadowshot, a proc will handily reapply at the higher value. For a bit of extra damage, in the second column there's Target Lock which stacks up to 5 times on repeated hits within 0.2s of each other, for an average ×1.2813 damage once you've emptied your mag. Or, for additional Void synergy, Destabilizing Rounds spreads Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 5.25s. Aegis ranks the Gun and Run origin trait as D-Tier for PvE - it grants up to ×3 stacks on kills for 11s, which after sprinting for 0.75s consumes stacks to reloads 25% of your magazine every 0.85s/applies a ×0.85 Draw Time scalar*, refreshing the buff duration. If you're interested, the name mirrors the Drifter’s traumatic past on the icy world of Eton, where he repeatedly starved to death before being revived by his Ghost, Blue. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-Champion PvE-EndGame PvE-God Void-Build M+KB Controller

// Gnawing Hunger - PvE Minor god AB 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Repulstor Brace, Demolitionist), (Destabilizing Rounds)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-Champion PvE-God Void-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for piercing Barrier Chmpions or clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly for Void builds. Gnawing Hunger is an adaptive auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "probably best overall auto archetype accounting for barrier intrinsic and damage profile". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just left vertical but loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For Void synergy, in the first trait perk column Repulsor Brace provides a ×0.5 damage resisting Void overshield for 10s after killing a void-debuffed enemy - the duration's boosted by ×1.5 with Echo of Persistence. For grenade builds, there's also Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column, Destabilizing Rounds spreads Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 5.25s. Aegis ranks the Gun and Run origin trait as D-Tier for PvE - it grants up to ×3 stacks on kills for 11s, which after sprinting for 0.75s consumes stacks to reloads 25% of your magazine every 0.85s/applies a ×0.85 Draw Time scalar*, refreshing the buff duration. If you're interested, the name mirrors the Drifter’s traumatic past on the icy world of Eton, where he repeatedly starved to death before being revived by his Ghost, Blue. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-Champion PvE-God Void-Build M+KB Controller

// Age-Old Bond - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forger Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Flared Magwell, Alloy Mag), (Demolitionist), (Golden Tricorn, Adrenaline Junkie)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd Grenade-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with grenade builds. Age-Old Bond is a high-impact auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "range outlier with good stagger access, 2nd best damage profile despite low fire rate". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, and ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical but tight and for this roll you need to use the barrel perk to control recoil to help hit crits so you could take the Recoil Direction to 100 or look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, look for perks that improve Stability, mag size or Reload Speed. For grenade builds, Demolitionist in the first trait perk column generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. You can use the extra grenades to help proc either Desperate Measures which provides up to ×3 stacks for max ×1.3 damage for a refreshable 7.5s for a weapon and then two ability kills or Adrenaline Junkie which stacks up to ×5 on kills for +20 handling and max ×1.33 damage, with a grenade kill giving an instant 5 stacks, for a refreshable 5s. Aegis ranks the Explosive Pact origin trait as D-Tier for PvE - it stacks up to ×5 on grenade ability use to boost Stability and Reload Speed for 7.5s. If you're interested, the name refers to the metaphysical, symbiotic relationship forged between a Guardian and an Ahamkara. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd Grenade-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Age-Old Bond - PvE Minor god AB 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forger Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Flared Magwell, Alloy Mag), (Meganeura, Dragonfly), (Rampage, Collective Action)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty. Age-Old Bond is a high-impact auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "range outlier with good stagger access, 2nd best damage profile despite low fire rate". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, and ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical but tight and for this roll you need to use the barrel perk to control recoil to help hit crits so you could take the Recoil Direction to 100 or look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, look for perks that improve Stability, mag size or Reload Speed. You can roll double damage perk combinations in the first trait perk column, look for Meganeura which builds up to ×3 stacks on crit hits for a refreshable 4s for Void AoE explosion in a 7.5m radius on a crit kill - the AoE damage scales with the number of stacks. Alternatively, there's Dragonfly procs a 4.5m [weapon energy matching] explosion on a crit kill. For both, the AoE damage is treated as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts, and also proc subclass fragments. In the second column for additional damage, Rampage stacks up to ×3 on kills for max ×1.331 damage for a refreshable 5s, with the stacks decaying one at a time or Collective Action boosts damage by ×1.2 after collecting an elemental pickup or throwing a strand tangle for 7s, refreshable up to a maximum 12s. Aegis ranks the Explosive Pact origin trait as D-Tier for PvE - it stacks up to ×5 on grenade ability usage to boost Stability and Reload Speed for 7.5s. If you're interested, the name refers to the metaphysical, symbiotic relationship forged between a Guardian and an Ahamkara. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Rufus's Fury - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Slice), (Paracausal Affinity, Hatchling)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God Strand-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Strand builds. Rufus's Fury is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just right of vertical and very loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For Strand builds, in the first trait perk column Slice procs for 9s after using a class ability, to Sever up to 5 enemies for ×0.6 damage reduction for a refreshable 10s. In the second column for damage, Paracausal Affinity boosts damage by ×1.2 for 6s after any kill of a Darkness energy type, or Hatchling spawns a Threadling on a crit kill or three crit hits within 3s of each other. Aegis ranks the Harmonic Resonance origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it boosts Handling and Reload Speed, and damage by up to a max ×1.1 against Dread and Lucent Hive Lightbearers for equipping other Root of Nightmares weapons. If you're interested, Rufus stems from the Latin word for "red" or "red-haired", which is possibly a thematic nod to the crimson-hued, Nightmare-infused aesthetics of Nezarec’s domain, while in-game lore establishes Rufus as one of his Acolytes. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Strand-Build M+KB Controller

// Rufus's Fury - PvE Minor god AB 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Demolitionist), (Adrenaline Junkie)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd Grenade-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly on grenade builds. Rufus's Fury is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just right of vertical and very loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For grenade builds, there's the classic trait perk combination of Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use and Adrenaline Junkie which stacks up to ×5 on kills for +20 handling and max ×1.33 damage, with a grenade kill giving an instant 5 stacks, for a refreshable 5s. Aegis ranks the Harmonic Resonance origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it boosts Handling and Reload Speed, and damage by up to a max ×1.1 against Dread and Lucent Hive Lightbearers for equipping other Root of Nightmares weapons. If you're interested, Rufus stems from the Latin word for "red" or "red-haired", which is possibly a thematic nod to the crimson-hued, Nightmare-infused aesthetics of Nezarec’s domain, while in-game lore establishes Rufus as one of his Acolytes. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd Grenade-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Rufus's Fury (Adept) - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Slice), (Paracausal Affinity, Hatchling)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God Strand-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Strand builds. Rufus's Fury (Adept) is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just right of vertical and very loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For Strand builds, in the first trait perk column Slice procs for 9s after using a class ability, to Sever up to 5 enemies for ×0.6 damage reduction for a refreshable 10s. In the second column for damage, Paracausal Affinity boosts damage by ×1.2 for 6s after any kill of a Darkness energy type, or Hatchling spawns a Threadling on a crit kill or three crit hits within 3s of each other. Aegis ranks the Harmonic Resonance origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it boosts Handling and Reload Speed, and damage by up to a max ×1.1 against Dread and Lucent Hive Lightbearers for equipping other Root of Nightmares weapons. If you're interested, Rufus stems from the Latin word for "red" or "red-haired", which is possibly a thematic nod to the crimson-hued, Nightmare-infused aesthetics of Nezarec’s domain, while in-game lore establishes Rufus as one of his Acolytes. // Rufus's Fury (Adept) - PvE Minor god AB 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Demolitionist), (Adrenaline Junkie)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd Grenade-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly on grenade builds. Rufus's Fury (Adept) is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just right of vertical and very loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For grenade builds, there's the classic trait perk combination of Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use and Adrenaline Junkie which stacks up to ×5 on kills for +20 handling and max ×1.33 damage, with a grenade kill giving an instant 5 stacks, for a refreshable 5s. Aegis ranks the Harmonic Resonance origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it boosts Handling and Reload Speed, and damage by up to a max ×1.1 against Dread and Lucent Hive Lightbearers for equipping other Root of Nightmares weapons. If you're interested, Rufus stems from the Latin word for "red" or "red-haired", which is possibly a thematic nod to the crimson-hued, Nightmare-infused aesthetics of Nezarec’s domain, while in-game lore establishes Rufus as one of his Acolytes. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd Grenade-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Adamantite - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Full Bore, Hammer-Forged Rifling, Smallbore, Extended Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Accurized Rounds, Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Reciprocity, Redirection, Physic), (Tear, Circle of Life)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Utility PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Adamantite is a support-frame auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "damage profile is serviceable, also has two niches in boosting damage and survivability". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, and it shoots tracking projectiles at enemies - any ability or weapon damage generates weapon energy that can be used when hipfiring at allies to heal them, with rapid healing propagating four Unravelling threads as per the Destiny Data Compendium - honestly, a bit disappointing if you were hoping for Woven Mail. On Support Frames, the Range stat determines how far your bullets will travel before vanishing, so look for that with the barrel perk. In the magazine perk slot, look for perks that improve Range, mag size or Reload Speed. In the first trait perk column, we've lost Slice in the reissued weapon, so we're left with Reciprocity which heals 3HP for each healing projectile that hits an ally or Redirection which stores damage by hitting red bar adds, for 2 stacks per hit up to 30 stacks, for use against orange and yellow bar enemies for ×1.5 damage at two stacks consumed per shot. For Strand synergy, there's also Physic which generates four Unravelling threads from both the user and ally, when the support frame boost is triggered. In the second column, synergising with the frame intrinsic, Circle of Life provides a ×1.25 damage boost for a refreshable 10s after rapidly healing allies. Aegis ranks the Heretical Behavior origin trait as S-Tier for PvE - it's excellent for additional damage, proccing for a refreshable 8s after 4 hits within 10s, or for 23s after being revived, to boost damage by ×1.08, and Handling and Reload Speed by +50 with a ×0.8 reload animation scalar. There's also the C-Tier Willing Vessel, which generates up to ×5 stacks of a slowly decaying buff on dealing damage and kills, to boost Stability, Handling and Reload Speed. If you're interested, the name is derived from the Greek adamas, meaning "unconquerable" or "untamed", historically used to describe a mythologically unbreakable mineral. Recommended MW: Range, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Utility PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// (Full Bore, Hammer-Forged Rifling, Smallbore, Extended Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Accurized Rounds, Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Demolitionist), (Desperate Measures)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Utility PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly for grenade builds. Adamantite is a support-frame auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "damage profile is serviceable, also has two niches in boosting damage and survivability". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, and it shoots tracking projectiles at enemies - any ability or weapon damage generates weapon energy that can be used when hipfiring at allies to heal them, with rapid healing propagating four Unravelling threads as per the Destiny Data Compendium - honestly, a bit disappointing if you were hoping for Woven Mail. On Support Frames, the Range stat determines how far your bullets will travel before vanishing, so look for that with the barrel perk. In the magazine perk slot, look for perks that improve Range, mag size or Reload Speed. For grenade build synergy and dealing with tougher individual adds, in the first trait perk column Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column, you can use those grenades with Desperate Measures which provides up to ×3 stacks for ×1.3 damage for a refreshable 7s (7.5s when enhanced) for a weapon and then two ability kills. Aegis ranks the Heretical Behavior origin trait as S-Tier for PvE - it's excellent for additional damage, proccing for a refreshable 8s after 4 hits within 10s, or for 23s after being revived, to boost damage by ×1.08, and Handling and Reload Speed by +50 with a ×0.8 reload animation scalar. There's also the C-Tier Willing Vessel, which generates up to ×5 stacks of a slowly decaying buff on dealing damage and kills, to boost Stability, Handling and Reload Speed. If you're interested, the name is derived from the Greek adamas, meaning "unconquerable" or "untamed", historically used to describe a mythologically unbreakable mineral. Recommended MW: Range, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Utility PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

Abyss Defiant is a high-impact auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "range outlier with good stagger access, 2nd best damage profile despite low fire rate". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, and ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is slightly left of vertical and tight so you can take your pick with the barrel perk to boost Handling, Stability or Range. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For a roll to clear both add groups, and which also synergises with Solar builds - Heal Clip provides ×2 Solar Cure (140 HP), and ×1 Cure on allies within 15m, when you finish a reload within ~5.5s after a kill, or Subsistence reloads 20% of the magazine on kills. All of the scorch stacks for both perks are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes. Aegis ranks the Cursed Thrall origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it synergises well with melee builds - killing an enemy with a melee will proc a 4.5m radius Cursed Thrall AoE explosion for 8.5s on the next weapon kill. // Abyss Defiant - PvE Minor god AB 2
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Pugilist, Proximity Power), (Incandescent, Swashbuckler, Collective Pugilism)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God Melee-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with melee builds. Abyss Defiant is a high-impact auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "range outlier with good stagger access, 2nd best damage profile despite low fire rate". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, and ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is slightly left of vertical and tight so you can take your pick with the barrel perk to boost Handling, Stability or Range. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For a roll to synergise with melee builds and the origin trait - Pugilist generates chunk melee ability energy on kills and +35 Handling for 3s after dealing melee damage, or Proximity Power stacks up ×3 times to provide +18 to your Melee attribute per stack on kills when another enemy's within 15m - a total a +54 for a refreshable 10s on further kills, so you can invest stat points into other armour attributes. In the second column, Incandescent spreads +30 Solar Scorch stacks within 4m on a red bar Minor add kill (+45 stacks within 8m for orange and yellow bars), with scorch damage counting as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts, and also proc subclass fragments and trait perks like Pugilism or Proximity Power. All of the scorch stacks for both perks are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes. There's also the more traditional choice of Swashbuckler, which generates up to ×5 damage stacks on kills for max ×1.33 damage, with a melee kill giving 5 stacks, for a refreshable 5s, or Collective Pugilism to grant melee energy for 3s, up to a maximum of 15s, on weapon kills after collecting or using an elemental pickup. Aegis ranks the Cursed Thrall origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it synergises well with melee builds - killing an enemy with a melee will proc a 4.5m radius Cursed Thrall AoE explosion for 8.5s on the next weapon kill. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God Melee-Build M+KB Controller

// Giver's Blessing - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Demolitionist), (Adrenaline Junkie)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God Grenade-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly for grenade builds. Giver's Blessing is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical and loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For a general purpose AR that'll help with tougher adds with grenade builds, in the first trait perk column Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column for damage, Adrenaline Junkie stacks up to ×5 on kills for +20 handling and max ×1.33 damage, with a grenade kill giving an instant 5 stacks, for a refreshable 5s. Aegis ranks the Exhaustive Research origin trait as B-Tier for PvE - it loads a micro-missile when ADSing for 0.6s at a target within 60m, with a 5.5s cooldown. If you're interested, the lore tab tells of an Eliksni Archon that murders a Dreg after obsessing over an Eliksni named Levaszk, who allegedly discovered something "beyond Ether" and "beyond time" hidden within dark matter. The "Giver" refers to this enigmatic cosmic source, and the "blessing" represents an ominous transcendence or ascension achieved through this dark matter discovery. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God Grenade-Build M+KB Controller

// Prosecutor - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Trickle Charge, Subsistence), (Gear Shift, Rolling Storm)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God Arc-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Arc builds. Prosecutor is a precision auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "worse damage profile than balanced, but has barrier intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, and a ×0.9 reduction in horizontal recoil, as per the Destiny Data Compendium. With the frame intrinsic, you don't really have to worry about Recoil Direction, so you can take your pick with the barrel perk to boost Handling, Stability or Range. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For Arc builds, in the first column to delay the need to reload Trickle Charge refills 10% of the magazine on receiving a stack of Arc Bolt Charge, and 20% when you discharge with a lightning bolt on a target, or Subsistence reloads 20% of the magazine on kills. In the second column for damage, Gear Shift stacks up to ×5 on reloads after kills or becoming Amplified for a refreshable 15s, granting up to a max ×1.35 damage boost, or for Arc target and AoE damage there's Rolling Storm to generate ×1 stack of Arc Bolt Charge (×2 when Amplified) on a kill, to trigger a lightning bolt at ×10 stacks. Aegis ranks the Crossing Over origin trait as F-Tier for PvE - it provides a little extra range and handling in the top half of the mag, and a small damage boost in the bottom starting at ×1.015 at 50% and ×1.03 with your penultimate and last shot. If you're interested, Prosecutor operates alongside other Prophecy Dungeon weapons like Adjudicator, Judgement, and Relentless to evoke a clinical, cosmic courtroom where The Nine evaluate humanity's worthiness. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God Arc-Build M+KB Controller

// Prosecutor - PvE Minor god AB 2
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Trickle Charge, Subsistence), (Target Lock, Onslaught)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Prosecutor is a precision auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "worse damage profile than balanced, but has barrier intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, and a ×0.9 reduction in horizontal recoil, as per the Destiny Data Compendium. With the frame intrinsic, you don't really have to worry about Recoil Direction, so you can take your pick with the barrel perk to boost Handling, Stability or Range. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. In the first column to delay the need to reload, on Arc builds Trickle Charge refills 10% of the magazine on receiving a stack of Arc Bolt Charge, and 20% when you discharge with a lightning bolt on a target, otherwise Subsistence reloads 20% of the magazine on kills. In the second column for damage, Target Lock stacks up to 5 times on repeated hits within 0.2s of each other, for up to ×1.4 damage at 110.33% of base magazine capacity - so works best with auto-refilled magazines or Onslaught, while not boosting damage per shot does increase raw DPS as you ramp up to the max ×3 stacks, for a refreshable 5s per kill, to increase RPM from 450 to around max 720. Aegis ranks the Crossing Over origin trait as F-Tier for PvE - it provides a little extra range and handling in the top half of the mag, and a small damage boost in the bottom starting at ×1.015 at 50% and ×1.03 with your penultimate and last shot. If you're interested, Prosecutor operates alongside other Prophecy Dungeon weapons like Adjudicator, Judgement, and Relentless to evoke a clinical, cosmic courtroom where The Nine evaluate humanity's worthiness. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Prosecutor - PvE Minor god AB 3
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Eddy Current, Light Touch, Enlightened Action), (Voltshot)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God Arc-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Arc builds. Prosecutor is a precision auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "worse damage profile than balanced, but has barrier intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, and a ×0.9 reduction in horizontal recoil, as per the Destiny Data Compendium. With the frame intrinsic, you don't really have to worry about Recoil Direction, so you can take your pick with the barrel perk to boost Handling, Stability or Range. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For Arc builds, in the first column to speed up reloads, Eddy Current boosts Handling with a ×0.95 animation scalar and +20 Reload Speed with a ×0.95 animation scalar for 3s after sprinting for 1.5s, which is all applied instantly with +60 to Reload Speed when Amplified, or Light Touch boosts Handling and Reload Speed, and provides an ADS scalar while you're Amplified (or Radiant, or Devour is active). In the second column for clearing groups of Minor adds, Voltshot applies Arc Jolt to the next target you hit within 8s, after a reload within 5.3s of a kill, for additional damage to the target on further hits plus AoE chain lightning damage on nearby enemies. Aegis ranks the Crossing Over origin trait as F-Tier for PvE - it provides a little extra range and handling in the top half of the mag, and a small damage boost in the bottom starting at ×1.015 at 50% and ×1.03 with your penultimate and last shot. If you're interested, Prosecutor operates alongside other Prophecy Dungeon weapons like Adjudicator, Judgement, and Relentless to evoke a clinical, cosmic courtroom where The Nine evaluate humanity's worthiness. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God Arc-Build M+KB Controller)

// Chrysura Melo - PvE Utility god AB 1
// (Full Bore, Hammer-Forged Rifling, Extended Barrel, Smallbore, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Rolling Storm), (Supercharged Magazine)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-Utility PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Arc-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly for Arc builds. Chrysura Melo is a support-frame auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "damage profile is serviceable, also has two niches in boosting damage and survivability". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, and it shoots tracking projectiles at enemies - any ability or weapon damage generates weapon energy that can be used when hipfiring at allies to heal them, with rapid healing also applying Amplified as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is just right of vertical and loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For Arc builds, in the first trait perk column Jolting Feedback applys Arc Jolt after 14 hits (11 when amplified) to tougher enemies who can survive the weapon damage, triggering chain lightning on further hits for both target and AoE damage. In the second, to delay reloads, Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the magazine every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff is active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity. Aegis ranks the Ignoble Deeds origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - inflicting an elemental debuff grants 2 stacks, up to a max of 10. One a kill, a stack is consumed to grant +35 to both Handling and Reload Speed until you run out of stacks. If you're interested, the name derives from Greek roots - Chrysura meaning "golden tail", which is a genus of parasitic cuckoo wasps, while Melo stems from "melos" meaning "song" or "melody" - combined, the name translates to "Parasitic Song". Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-Utility PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Arc-Build M+KB Controller

// Zealous Ideal - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Enhanced Heatsink, Ionized Heatsink, Accelerated Heatsink, Light Heatsink, Tactical Heatsink), (Cooling Baubles), (Onslaught)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Solar builds. Zealous Ideal is a balanced heat auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "strict downgrade from adaptive". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, and is like an extra perk set, first providing reduced heat generation, significantly increasing the number of shots before overheating, and providing a pseudo-Reconstruction, automatically "refilling" as the weapon loses heat when not being used. Additionally, there's an intrinsic ×1.15 damage boost against orange and yellow bars and vehicles, plus a ×2 vent rate if you manually reload when the heat bar is at >87.5% of maximum heat to proc Perfect Vent, as per the Destiny Data Compendium. With the Recoil Direction already at 95, you can take your pick with the barrel perk to boost Handling, Stability or Range. In the battery perk slot, look for something that reduces heat generation, or improves cooling efficiency or vent speed. With a Solar subclass, you can use Embers of Tempering and Mercy to generate Firesprites which grant Restoration. In the first trait perk slot, Cooling Baubles reduces weapon heat by 33% for each Elemental pickup, such as your Firesprites, or power orb collected. In the second column, Onslaught, while not boosting damage per shot does increase raw DPS as you ramp up to the max ×3 stacks, for a refreshable 5s per kill, to increase RPM from 450 to around max 720, working best when you avoid reloading. Aegis ranks the Imperial Allegiance origin trait as A-Tier for PvE if you're in a fireteam - it stacks up to ×3 per ally within 15m, boosting cooling by a max ×1.105 and increasing damage when above 50% heat by max ×1.105. If you're interested, this is modelled on the E-11 medium blaster rifle, the standard-issue weapon for the Galactic Empire's stormtroopers, while the name refers to the rigid, uncompromising pursuit of a perfect standard or cause. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Lethal Abundance - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Rewind Rounds), (Onslaught, One For All, Target Lock)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Lethal Abundance is a high-impact auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "range outlier with good stagger access, 2nd best damage profile despite low fire rate". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, and ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is slightly left of vertical and tight so you could take the Recoil Direction to 100 with the barrel perk or look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For a general-purpose roll, Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the magazine empties. Use this to keep shooting with Onslaught in the second column which, while not boosting damage per shot, does increase raw DPS as you ramp up to the max ×3 stacks, for a refreshable 5s per kill, to increase RPM from 400 to around max 640. For straight damage, One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 11s after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each, or Target Lock stacks up to 5 times on repeated hits within 0.2s of each other, for up to ×1.4 damage at 110.33% of base magazine capacity - so works best on overflowed or auto-refilled magazines. Aegis ranks the Skulking Wolf origin trait as A-Tier for PvE which, after you get a kill at ≤90 HP, enhances radar and makes adds less accurate when firing at you for 10s - similar to when you're Amplified. If you're interested, this was originally introduced during Season of the Wish, with the name implying the concept of too many Ahamkara wishes becoming a deadly curse. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Reckless Oracle - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Destabilizing Rounds, Demolitionist), (Demoralize)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God Void-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Void builds. Reckless Oracle is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical and loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For Void builds, in the first trait perk column Destabilizing Rounds spreads Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 5.25s. For grenade builds, there's also Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column, Demoralize spreads a ~4m AoE cloud of Void Weaken for a ×1.15 boost to damage for your fireteam for 5s on a crit kill, which you can use with Echoes of Harvest and Starvation to continuously proc Devour. Aegis ranks the Photoinhibition origin trait as B-Tier for PvE - it boosts shield, overshield and barrier damage, disorienting for 5s on a shield break, and AoE disorienting enemies in a 5m radius on a matching Void shield break. If you're interested, the name draws a parallel between the Vex's fatalistic timeline-weaving mechanics and the tragic hubris of the Kentarch 3 - a fireteam of Guardians who accepted Dark powers within the Black Garden, representing the dangerous folly of pursuing forbidden, absolute knowledge while actively accelerating toward a grim, predetermined fate. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God Void-Build M+KB Controller

// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Destabilizing Rounds, Demolitionist), (One For All)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Reckless Oracle is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical and loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For a general purpose PvE roll, in the first trait perk column Destabilizing Rounds spreads Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 5.25s, and synergises with Void builds. For grenade builds, there's also Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column for damage, One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 11s after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each. Aegis ranks the Photoinhibition origin trait as B-Tier for PvE - it boosts shield, overshield and barrier damage, disorienting for 5s on a shield break, and AoE disorienting enemies in a 5m radius on a matching Void shield break. If you're interested, the name draws a parallel between the Vex's fatalistic timeline-weaving mechanics and the tragic hubris of the Kentarch 3 - a fireteam of Guardians who accepted Dark powers within the Black Garden, representing the dangerous folly of pursuing forbidden, absolute knowledge while actively accelerating toward a grim, predetermined fate. // Reckless Oracle - PvE Minor god AB 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Destabilizing Rounds, Demolitionist), (Collective Action, Paracausal Affinity)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God Void-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty. Reckless Oracle is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical and loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For a general purpose PvE roll, in the first trait perk column Destabilizing Rounds spreads Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 5.25s, and synergises with Void builds. For grenade builds, there's also Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column for damage, Collective Action boosts damage by ×1.2 after collecting an elemental pickup or throwing a strand tangle for 7s, refreshable up to a maximum 12s, or Paracausal Affinity boosts damage by ×1.2 for a refreshable 6s after any kill of a Light energy type. Aegis ranks the Photoinhibition origin trait as B-Tier for PvE - it boosts shield, overshield and barrier damage, disorienting for 5s on a shield break, and AoE disorienting enemies in a 5m radius on a matching Void shield break. If you're interested, the name draws a parallel between the Vex's fatalistic timeline-weaving mechanics and the tragic hubris of the Kentarch 3 - a fireteam of Guardians who accepted Dark powers within the Black Garden, representing the dangerous folly of pursuing forbidden, absolute knowledge while actively accelerating toward a grim, predetermined fate. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God Void-Build M+KB Controller

// Arctic Haze - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Incandescent), (Frenzy)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Solar-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Solar builds. Arctic Haze is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical and very loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For a roll to clear group adds, and which also synergises with Solar builds, in the first trait perk column, Incandescent spreads +30 Solar Scorch stacks within 4m on a red bar Minor add kill (+45 stacks within 8m for orange and yellow bars), with scorch damage counting as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts, and also proc subclass fragments. All of the scorch stacks are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes. In the second column for damage, Frenzy after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s, until you're out of combat again. Aegis ranks the Winterized Gear origin trait as F-Tier for PvE - it provides ×3 stacks after 12s out of combat - each stack decays after 5s in combat providing a gradually reducing boost to Stability, Handling and Reload Speed. If you're interested, the name functions as a literal description of its frozen, storm-swept source world of Europa. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Solar-Build M+KB Controller

// Arctic Haze - PvE Minor god AB 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Heal Clip), (Frenzy)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Arctic Haze is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical and very loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For an all-purpose add-clearing roll, in the first trait perk column Heal Clip provides ×2 Solar Cure (140 HP), and ×1 Cure on allies within 15m, when you finish a reload within ~5.5s after a kill. In the second column for damage, Frenzy after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s, until you're out of combat again. Aegis ranks the Winterized Gear origin trait as F-Tier for PvE - it provides ×3 stacks after 12s out of combat - each stack decays after 5s in combat providing a gradually reducing boost to Stability, Handling and Reload Speed. If you're interested, the name functions as a literal description of its frozen, storm-swept source world of Europa. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Pluperfect - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Stopping Power, Rewind Rounds, Demolitionist, Enlightened Action), (Kinetic Tremors, Adhesive Ordnance)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Pluperfect is a high-impact auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "range outlier with good stagger access, 2nd best damage profile despite low fire rate". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, and ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is left of vertical and tight so you could take the Recoil Direction to 100 with the barrel perk or look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks - particularly High-Caliber Rounds - work well for this roll, or perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed are the best alternatives. For a general purpose kinetic AR in end-game content, you have a range of options available. In the first trait perk column, Stopping Power substantially improves stagger against enemies, as well as boosting damage at the bottom of the mag, there's Rewind Rounds to expand your effective mag size, Demolitionist for chunk grenade energy and instant ammo refills on grenade use, or Enlightened Action to boost Reload Speed and Handling for just landing hits. In the second column, for damage, Kinetic Tremors emits three 6m AoE Kinetic shockwaves after 11 hits within 3s of each other, which are treated as weapon damage so inherit damage boosts from Stopping Power. Alternatively, if you're using Kinetic Synthesis from the Encrypted Data Disk artifact, you can pick up the kinetic bricks to proc either Adhesive Ordnance which generates a Sticky Bomb from thin air when you pickup an ammo brick, which detonates 0.8s after impact for 6m AoE damage plus applying exhaust for 5s - again the explosion is treated as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts. Aegis ranks the Fail Deadly origin trait as A-Tier for PvE, granting a stack per damage tick up to the base magazine size - at max stacks a reload provides ×2 magazine size, which stacks with other mag size buffs like Rewind Rounds, as well as boosting Range by +15 and Aim Assist by +5. If you're interested, the name originates from the Latin phrase "plus quam perfectum", which translates literally to "more than perfect". Pluperfect is also a verb tense (also known as the past perfect), describing an action that was completed before another event in the past, such as "By the time we landed for the Taniks checkpoint, Adams had already equipped Divinity". Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Pluperfect - PvE Minor god AB 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Stopping Power, Rewind Rounds, Demolitionist, Enlightened Action), (All-Star)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Pluperfect is a high-impact auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "range outlier with good stagger access, 2nd best damage profile despite low fire rate". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, and ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is left of vertical and tight so you could take the Recoil Direction to 100 with the barrel perk or look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks - particularly High-Caliber Rounds - work well for this roll, or perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed are the best alternatives. For a general purpose kinetic AR in end-game content, you have a range of options available. In the first trait perk column, Stopping Power substantially improves stagger against enemies, as well as boosting damage at the bottom of the mag, there's Rewind Rounds to expand your effective mag size, Demolitionist for chunk grenade energy and instant ammo refills on grenade use, or Enlightened Action to boost Reload Speed and Handling for just landing hits. In the second column, for damage, Kinetic Tremors emits three 6m AoE Kinetic shockwaves after 11 hits within 3s of each other, which are treated as weapon damage so inherit damage boosts from Stopping Power. Alternatively, if you're using Kinetic Synthesis in the Encrypted Data Disk artifact, you can pick up the kinetic bricks to proc All-Star which boosts damage by ×1.35 and Reload Speed by +100 for a refreshable 7s after picking up a special or kinetic brick, or 10s for a heavy brick, with the timer starting only after you ready. Aegis ranks the Fail Deadly origin trait as A-Tier for PvE, granting a stack per damage tick up to the base magazine size - at max stacks a reload provides ×2 magazine size, which stacks with other mag size buffs like Rewind Rounds, as well as boosting Range by +15 and Aim Assist by +5. If you're interested, the name originates from the Latin phrase "plus quam perfectum", which translates literally to "more than perfect". Pluperfect is also a verb tense (also known as the past perfect), describing an action that was completed before another event in the past, such as "By the time we landed for the Taniks checkpoint, Adams had already equipped Divinity". Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Pluperfect - PvE Minor god AB 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Demolitionist), (Adrenaline Junkie)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God Grenade-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with grenade builds. Pluperfect is a high-impact auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "range outlier with good stagger access, 2nd best damage profile despite low fire rate". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable, and ×0.8 accuracy cone size and ×0.83 accuracy cone growth when moving 2.8m/s or less (crouch walking speed) as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is left of vertical and tight so you could take the Recoil Direction to 100 with the barrel perk or look to boost Stability to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks - particularly High-Caliber Rounds - work well for this roll, or perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed are the best alternatives. For grenade builds, there's the classic trait perk combination of Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use, and Adrenaline Junkie which stacks up to ×5 on kills for +20 handling and max ×1.33 damage, with a grenade kill giving an instant 5 stacks, for a refreshable 5s. Aegis ranks the Fail Deadly origin trait as A-Tier for PvE, granting a stack per damage tick up to the base magazine size - at max stacks a reload provides ×2 magazine size, which stacks with other mag size buffs like Rewind Rounds, as well as boosting Range by +15 and Aim Assist by +5. If you're interested, the name originates from the Latin phrase "plus quam perfectum", which translates literally to "more than perfect". Pluperfect is also a verb tense (also known as the past perfect), describing an action that was completed before another event in the past, such as "By the time we landed for the Taniks checkpoint, Adams had already equipped Divinity". Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God Grenade-Build M+KB Controller

// Seventh Seraph Carbine - PvE Utility god AB 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Seraph Rounds, Tactical Mag Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Attrition Orbs, Stopping Power), (Frenzy)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Utility PvE-Minor PvE-Champion PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for both dealing with Barrier Champions and clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Seventh Seraph Carbine is a precision auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "worse damage profile than balanced, but has barrier intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, and a ×0.9 reduction in horizontal recoil, as per the Destiny Data Compendium. With the frame intrinsic, you don't really have to worry about Recoil Direction, so you can take your pick with the barrel perk to boost Handling, Stability or Range. In the magazine perk slot, Seraph Rounds is the clear stand-out, offering the benefits of High-Caliber, Armor-Piercing and Ricochet Rounds, otherwise perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size also work. Barrier champions are a little more difficult deal with at range in the final sandbox so, for a roll that can fill that role as well dealing with tougher Minors, in the first trait perk column Attrition Orbs spawns a power orb after ~19 hits within 5s of each other to proc armour mods, or Stopping Power provides increased stagger, and increased damage on low health enemies - ×1.07 when health is below 34%, ×1.1 when <23% and ×1.15 when <18%. In the second column, Frenzy after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s. Aegis ranks the Rasputin's Arsenal origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it refills 60% of your magazine on breaking an enemy shield or barrier, but it is useful on a weapon roll meant for a Barrier Champion. There's also the A-Tier Eyes Up, which is a bit inconsistent to proc, granting up to ×15 stacks to improve Range, Reload Speed and boost damage by a max ×1.05 after a kill when in combat for a 8s, extendable to 25s on further kills. If you're interested, the name reflects Bungie's deep-rooted obsession with religious symbolism and the number seven. In Christian theology, Seraphim are the highest order of angels who sit directly at the throne of God, traditionally described as celestial beings of pure fire with six wings. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Utility PvE-Minor PvE-Champion PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Seventh Seraph Carbine - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Seraph Rounds, Tactical Mag Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Fourth Time's the Charm, Ambitious Assassin), (Target Lock)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for both clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Seventh Seraph Carbine is a precision auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "worse damage profile than balanced, but has barrier intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, and a ×0.9 reduction in horizontal recoil, as per the Destiny Data Compendium. With the frame intrinsic, you don't really have to worry about Recoil Direction, so you can take your pick with the barrel perk to boost Handling, Stability or Range. In the magazine perk slot, Seraph Rounds is the clear stand-out, offering the benefits of High-Caliber, Armor-Piercing and Ricochet Rounds, otherwise perks that improve Reload Speed or mag size also work. For dealing with tougher Minor adds, in the first trait perk slot, Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 3s of each other, almost doubling your effective mag size if you can keep hitting crits, or Ambitious Assassin builds up to ×15 stacks on kills within 8s of each other - on reload, each stack overflows 10% of the magazine, so at max stacks that's ×2.5 base capacity, rounding up. In the second column, for damage, Target Lock stacks up to 5 times on repeated hits within 0.2s of each other, for up to ×1.4 damage at 110.33% of base magazine capacity - so works best on overflowed or auto-refilled magazines. Aegis ranks the Eyes Up origin trait as A-Tier for PvE, although the procs are a bit inconsistent, it grants up to ×15 stacks to improve Range, Reload Speed and boost damage by a max ×1.05 after a kill when in combat for a 8s, extendable to 25s on further kills. Otherwise, the C-Tier Rasputin's Arsenal refills 60% of your magazine on breaking an enemy shield or barrier. If you're interested, the name reflects Bungie's deep-rooted obsession with religious symbolism and the number seven. In Christian theology, Seraphim are the highest order of angels who sit directly at the throne of God, traditionally described as celestial beings of pure fire with six wings. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// (Full Bore, Hammer-Forged Rifling, Extended Barrel, Smallbore, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Demoralizing Rounds), (Destabilizing Rounds)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-Utility PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God Void-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly for Void builds. Cusp Sempiternal is a support-frame auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "damage profile is serviceable, also has two niches in boosting damage and survivability". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, and it shoots tracking projectiles at enemies - any ability or weapon damage generates weapon energy that can be used when hipfiring at allies to heal them, with rapid healing also applying Amplified as per the Destiny Data Compendium. On Support Frames, the Range stat determines how far your bullets will travel before vanishing, so look for that with the barrel perk. In the magazine perk slot, look for perks that improve Range, mag size or Reload Speed. For Void builds, Demoralize spreads a ~4m AoE cloud of Void Weaken for a ×1.15 boost to damage for your fireteam for 5s on a crit kill, which you can use with Echoes of Harvest and Starvation to continuously proc Devour. In the second column for more AoE damage, Destabilizing Rounds spreads Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 5.25s. Aegis ranks the Frame of Reference origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it stacks up to ×5 times on kills, providing up to ×1.07 damage and boosts to Reload Speed and Range for 8s, with the timer pausing when the weapon's stowed. If you're interested, "Cusp" is a point of transition, a threshold, or a sharp apex where two curves meet, and "Sempiternal" translates from Latin to mean ever-lasting, continuous, or of never-ending duration within, rather than outside of time. This reflects the ultimate Vex goal of reaching the absolute threshold of time to cement a permanent, unalterable victory where they become a fundamental, everlasting law of the universe. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-Utility PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God Void-Build M+KB Controller
