// Intercalary - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Demolitionist), (Desperate Measures)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd Grenade-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with grenade builds. Intercalary is an adaptive auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "probably best overall auto archetype accounting for barrier intrinsic and damage profile". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical and loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For grenade builds, in the first trait perk column Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column, you can use your grenades to proc Desperate Measures which provides up to ×3 stacks for max ×1.3 damage for a refreshable 7.5s for a weapon and then two ability kills. Aegis ranks the Frame of Reference origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it stacks up to ×5 times on kills, providing up to ×1.07 damage and boosts to Reload Speed and Range for 8s, with the timer pausing when the weapon's stowed. If you're interested, the name is derived from the Latin intercalārius, combining inter ("between") and calāre ("to call out or proclaim"), and ties into Desert Perpetual's time-themed naming. An "intercalary day", better known as a leap day, is artificially inserted into a calendar to keep human timekeeping aligned with natural astronomical cycles. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd Grenade-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Intercalary - PvE Minor god AB 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Demolitionist), (One For All)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Intercalary is an adaptive auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "probably best overall auto archetype accounting for barrier intrinsic and damage profile". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical and loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For tackling tougher Minor adds, in the first trait perk column Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column for damage, One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 11s after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each. Aegis ranks the Frame of Reference origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it stacks up to ×5 times on kills, providing up to ×1.07 damage and boosts to Reload Speed and Range for 8s, with the timer pausing when the weapon's stowed. If you're interested, the name is derived from the Latin intercalārius, combining inter ("between") and calāre ("to call out or proclaim"), and ties into Desert Perpetual's time-themed naming. An "intercalary day", better known as a leap day, is artificially inserted into a calendar to keep human timekeeping aligned with natural astronomical cycles. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Intercalary - PvE Minor god AB 5
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Rewind Rounds, Subsistence), (Onslaught)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty. Intercalary is an adaptive auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "probably best overall auto archetype accounting for barrier intrinsic and damage profile". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical and loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For tackling tougher Minor adds, in the first trait perk column Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the magazine empties, or Subsistence reloads 20% of the magazine on kills. In the second column for damage, Onslaught, while not boosting damage per shot does increase raw DPS as you ramp up to the max ×3 stacks, for a refreshable 5s per kill, to increase RPM from 600 to around max 960. Aegis ranks the Frame of Reference origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it stacks up to ×5 times on kills, providing up to ×1.07 damage and boosts to Reload Speed and Range for 8s, with the timer pausing when the weapon's stowed. If you're interested, the name is derived from the Latin intercalārius, combining inter ("between") and calāre ("to call out or proclaim"), and ties into Desert Perpetual's time-themed naming. An "intercalary day", better known as a leap day, is artificially inserted into a calendar to keep human timekeeping aligned with natural astronomical cycles. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Scathelocke - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Mag Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Rewind Rounds, Subsistence), (Kinetic Tremors)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for both dealing with Barrier Champions and clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Scathelocke is an adaptive auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "probably best overall auto archetype accounting for barrier intrinsic and damage profile". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. With the frame intrinsic, you don't really have to worry about Recoil Direction, so you can take your pick with the barrel perk to boost Handling, Stability or Range. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed are the best available. Barrier Champions are a little more difficult deal with at range in the final sandbox so, for a roll that can fill that role as well as help with dealing with Minor add groups, in the first trait perk column Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the magazine empties or Subsistence reloads 20% of the magazine on kills. In the second column, Kinetic Tremors emits three 6m AoE Kinetic shockwaves after 11 hits within 3s of each other, which are treated as weapon damage so inherit damage boosts and also proc weapon effects like Attrition Orbs' orb generation and Shoot to Loot's ammo and orb pickup. Aegis ranks the Veteran's Wisdom origin trait as S-Tier for PvE - it procs on a crit kill or 3 kills within 3s of each other to generate an 9m AoE kinetic shockwave which inflicts Exhaust on enemies. There's also the C-Tier Field Tested, which starts subtly but if you can stay alive to get hits and kills, you'll start to feel the boosts to Range, Stability, Handling and Reload Speed as you get to the higher stacks. If you're interested, Scathelocke is an alternate name for Will Scarlet, one of Robin Hood's legendary "Merry Men" in English folklore. It's a compound of the Old Norse/ Middle English word "scathe" (meaning to injure, harm, or obliterate) and "lock" (as in a lock of hair). Historically, this surname was often given as a nickname to an aggressive fighter or an incompetent barber. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Scathelocke - PvE Minor god AB 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Mag Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Attrition Orbs, Rewind Rounds, Subsistence, Shoot to Loot), (One for All, All-Star)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Scathelocke is an adaptive auto rifle, which are rated B-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "probably best overall auto archetype accounting for barrier intrinsic and damage profile". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. With the frame intrinsic, you don't really have to worry about Recoil Direction, so you can take your pick with the barrel perk to boost Handling, Stability or Range. In the magazine perk slot, perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed are the best available. For a kinetic AR to take on tougher adds, in the first trait perk column, for utility, Attrition Orbs spawns a power orb after ~19 hits within 5s of each other to proc armour mods, or Shoot to Loot lets you pick up remote ammo bricks or orbs from safety by shooting them, which also reloads all your weapons from reserves. Or, for larger effective mag size, Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the magazine empties or Subsistence reloads 20% of the magazine on kills. In the second column for damage, One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 11s after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each or All-Star boosts damage by ×1.35 and Reload Speed by +100 for a refreshable 7s after picking up a special brick or kinetic brick dropped by the Kinetic Synthesis artefact mod, or 10s for a heavy brick, with the timer starting only after you ready. Aegis ranks the Veteran's Wisdom origin trait as S-Tier for PvE - it procs on a crit kill or 3 kills within 3s of each other to generate an 9m AoE kinetic shockwave which inflicts Exhaust on enemies. There's also the C-Tier Field Tested, which starts subtly but if you can stay alive to get hits and kills, you'll start to feel the boosts to Range, Stability, Handling and Reload Speed as you get to the higher stacks. If you're interested, Scathelocke is an alternate name for Will Scarlet, one of Robin Hood's legendary "Merry Men" in English folklore. It's a compound of the Old Norse/ Middle English word "scathe" (meaning to injure, harm, or obliterate) and "lock" (as in a lock of hair). Historically, this surname was often given as a nickname to an aggressive fighter or an incompetent barber. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Positive Outlook - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Repulsor Brace), (Destabilizing Rounds)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd Void-Build PvE-God Arc-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Void builds. Positive Outlook is a precision auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "worse damage profile than balanced, but has barrier intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, and a ×0.9 reduction in horizontal recoil, as per the Destiny Data Compendium. With the frame intrinsic, you don't really have to worry about Recoil Direction, so you can take your pick with the barrel perk to boost Handling, Stability or Range. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For Void builds, there's the classic trait perk pairing of Repulsor Brace to provide a ×0.5 damage resisting Void overshield for 10s after killing a void-debuffed enemy - the duration's boosted by ×1.5 with Echo of Persistence, and Destabilizing Rounds to spread Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 5.25s. If you're lucky you'll drop a shiny version with Accelerated Assault origin trait, which Aegis ranks as S-Tier for PvE - it passively refills 10% of the magazine, and boosts damage by ×1.05 for getting 3 hits within a 1.6s window to delay reloads further. If you don't get a shiny drop, there's the B-Tier Vanguard Determination which provides up to ×3 stacks of damage resistance to max 6% - that's ×0.94 incoming damage - for a refreshable 12.5s, on kills within 20s of using a class ability. Alternatively there's the D-Tier Omolon Fluid Dynamics, which boosts Stability by up to +20 and Reload Speed by up to +30 in the top half of the mag, decreasing linearly until the mag's at 50%. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd Void-Build PvE-God Arc-Build M+KB Controller

// Veiled Threat - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Shoot to Loot, Attrition Orbs), (Collective Action)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God Stasis-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Stasis builds. Veiled Threat is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is vertical and very loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For Stasis builds, in the first trait perk column Shoot to Loot lets you pick up remote ammo bricks or orbs from safety by shooting them, which also reloads all your weapons from reserves or Attrition Orbs spawns a power orb after 11 hits within 5s of each other to proc armour mods. In the second column using Whispers of Chill and Conduction with Collective Action boosts damage by ×1.2 after collecting a Stasis shard, other elemental pickup or throwing a strand tangle for 7s, refreshable up to a maximum 12s. Aegis ranks the Radiolaria Transposer origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it's useful for clearing adds - three Minors or one Major or Boss enemy kill will result in a 3m AoE explosion on death, leaving a damaging pool of radiolaria for a few seconds. The damage is treated as weapon damage so inherits damage boosts and also procs weapon effects like Attrition Orbs' orb generation or picking up ammo bricks and power orbs with Shoot to Loot. If you're interested, the name serves as a double entendre, referring to a hidden or disguised danger but also evoking the Veil. It was introduced during Episode: Echoes alongside a mission named Veiled Threats (where Sol Divisive Vex attempt to exploit a lingering connection to the Veil) - the name highlights a lingering, hidden malice that persists even after the Witness's defeat. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Veiled Threat - PvE Minor god AB 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Shoot to Loot, Attrition Orbs, Threat Detector), (Surrounded)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty. Veiled Threat is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is vertical and very loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For an all-round roll for CQC use, the first trait perk column Shoot to Loot lets you pick up remote ammo bricks or orbs from safety by shooting them, which also reloads all your weapons from reserves or Attrition Orbs spawns a power orb after 11 hits within 5s of each other to proc armour mods. Alternatively, for better gunfeel, Threat Detector procs per enemy within 15m up to ×2 stacks, with two stacks providing +44 Stability, +64 Reload Speed with a ×0.81 animation scalar and +100 Handling. In the second column Surrounded boosts damage by up to to ×1.47 while within 15m of 3+ enemies. Aegis ranks the Radiolaria Transposer origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it's useful for clearing adds - three Minors or one Major or Boss enemy kill will result in a 3m AoE explosion on death, leaving a damaging pool of radiolaria for a few seconds. The damage is treated as weapon damage so inherits damage boosts and also procs weapon effects like Attrition Orbs' orb generation or picking up ammo bricks and power orbs with Shoot to Loot. If you're interested, the name serves as a double entendre, referring to a hidden or disguised danger but also evoking the Veil. It was introduced during Episode: Echoes alongside a mission named Veiled Threats (where Sol Divisive Vex attempt to exploit a lingering connection to the Veil) - the name highlights a lingering, hidden malice that persists even after the Witness's defeat. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Destabilizing Rounds), (Repulsor Brace)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God Void-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Void builds. Reckless Oracle is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical and loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For Void builds, there's the classic trait perk pairing of Destabilizing Rounds to spread Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 5.25s and Repulsor Brace to provide a ×0.5 damage resisting Void overshield for 10s after killing a void-debuffed enemy - the duration's boosted by ×1.5 with Echo of Persistence. Aegis ranks the Elliptical Orbit origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist, which changes to Outer Orbit for a refreshable 3s after sprinting for 1.5s which significantly boosts Ammo Generation. There's also the very handy B-Tier Photoinhibition which is excellent in PvE, boosting shield, overshield and barrier damage, disorienting for 5s on a shield break, and AoE disorienting enemies in a 5m radius on a matching Void shield break. If you're interested, the name draws a parallel between the Vex's fatalistic timeline-weaving mechanics and the tragic hubris of the Kentarch 3 - a fireteam of Guardians who accepted Dark powers within the Black Garden, representing the dangerous folly of pursuing forbidden, absolute knowledge while actively accelerating toward a grim, predetermined fate. In the second column, for damage, One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 11s after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each or Chaos Reshaped stacks up to ×2 on dealing or receiving damage within a 5s window for at least 11.5s - at ×1 it provides a ×1.2 damage boost for a refreshable 7s and then at ×2 provides ×1.35 damage for a refreshable 7s together with a healing burst of up to 35HP that also starts health regeneration - although this is bugged, so while it's active it interrupts natural recovery and prevents regenerating the final 20-25% of your health bar. Aegis ranks the Elliptical Orbit origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist, which changes to Outer Orbit for a refreshable 3s after sprinting for 1.5s which significantly boosts Ammo Generation. // Reckless Oracle - PvE Minor god AB 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Proximity Power), (Swashbuckler)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd Melee-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with melee builds. Reckless Oracle is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical and loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For melee builds, in the first trait perk column Proximity Power stacks up ×3 times to provide +18 to your Melee attribute per stack on kills when another enemy's within 15m - a total a +54 for a refreshable 10s on further kills, so you can invest stat points into other armour attributes. You can pair that with Swashbuckler in the second column to generate up to ×5 damage stacks on kills for max ×1.33 damage, with a melee kill giving 5 stacks, for a refreshable 5s. Aegis ranks the Elliptical Orbit origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist, which changes to Outer Orbit for a refreshable 3s after sprinting for 1.5s which significantly boosts Ammo Generation. There's also the very handy B-Tier Photoinhibition which is excellent in PvE, boosting shield, overshield and barrier damage, disorienting for 5s on a shield break, and AoE disorienting enemies in a 5m radius on a matching Void shield break. If you're interested, the name draws a parallel between the Vex's fatalistic timeline-weaving mechanics and the tragic hubris of the Kentarch 3 - a fireteam of Guardians who accepted Dark powers within the Black Garden, representing the dangerous folly of pursuing forbidden, absolute knowledge while actively accelerating toward a grim, predetermined fate. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd Melee-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Reckless Oracle - PvE Minor god AB 4
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Collective Demolition, Fourth Time's the Charm, Subsistence), (Paracausal Affinity)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty. Reckless Oracle is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical and loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For a general purpose roll for clearing tougher Minor adds, in the first trait perk column Collective Demolition synergises with grenade builds by providing grenade ability energy on hits after interacting with an elemental pickup - Firesprites and Void Breaches for 8s, Ionic Traces, Stasis Shards for 4s and Strand Tangles and Arc Elemental Orbs 4s each for pickup and explosion when thrown. Otherwise Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 3s of each other, delaying the need to reload and almost doubling mag size if you can keep hitting crits or Subsistence reloads 20% of the magazine on kills, again delaying the need to reload. In the second column, for damage, there's Paracausal Affinity which boosts damage by ×1.2 for a refreshable 6s after any kill of a Light energy type. Aegis ranks the Elliptical Orbit origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist, which changes to Outer Orbit for a refreshable 3s after sprinting for 1.5s which significantly boosts Ammo Generation. There's also the very handy B-Tier Photoinhibition which is excellent in PvE, boosting shield, overshield and barrier damage, disorienting for 5s on a shield break, and AoE disorienting enemies in a 5m radius on a matching Void shield break. If you're interested, the name draws a parallel between the Vex's fatalistic timeline-weaving mechanics and the tragic hubris of the Kentarch 3 - a fireteam of Guardians who accepted Dark powers within the Black Garden, representing the dangerous folly of pursuing forbidden, absolute knowledge while actively accelerating toward a grim, predetermined fate. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Reckless Oracle - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Destabilizing Rounds), (Repulsor Brace)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God Void-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Void builds. Reckless Oracle is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical and loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For Void builds, there's the classic trait perk pairing of Destabilizing Rounds to spread Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 5.25s and Repulsor Brace to provide a ×0.5 damage resisting Void overshield for 10s after killing a void-debuffed enemy - the duration's boosted by ×1.5 with Echo of Persistence. Aegis ranks the Elliptical Orbit origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist, which changes to Outer Orbit for a refreshable 3s after sprinting for 1.5s which significantly boosts Ammo Generation. There's also the very handy B-Tier Photoinhibition which is excellent in PvE, boosting shield, overshield and barrier damage, disorienting for 5s on a shield break, and AoE disorienting enemies in a 5m radius on a matching Void shield break. If you're interested, the name draws a parallel between the Vex's fatalistic timeline-weaving mechanics and the tragic hubris of the Kentarch 3 - a fireteam of Guardians who accepted Dark powers within the Black Garden, representing the dangerous folly of pursuing forbidden, absolute knowledge while actively accelerating toward a grim, predetermined fate. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God Void-Build M+KB Controller

For a general purpose roll for clearing tougher Minor adds, in the first trait perk column Subsistence reloads 20% of the magazine on kills, delaying the need to reload. In the second column, for damage, One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 11s after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each or Chaos Reshaped stacks up to ×2 on dealing or receiving damage within a 5s window for at least 11.5s - at ×1 it provides a ×1.2 damage boost for a refreshable 7s and then at ×2 provides ×1.35 damage for a refreshable 7s together with a healing burst of up to 35HP that also starts health regeneration - although this is bugged, so while it's active it interrupts natural recovery and prevents regenerating the final 20-25% of your health bar. Aegis ranks the Elliptical Orbit origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist, which changes to Outer Orbit for a refreshable 3s after sprinting for 1.5s which significantly boosts Ammo Generation. // Reckless Oracle - PvE Minor god AB 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Proximity Power), (Swashbuckler)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd Melee-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with melee builds. Reckless Oracle is a rapid-fire auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "solid damage profile but a strict step down compared to adaptive in same range tier". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Overload, better total ammo reserves and a ×0.8 reload animation scalar when the magazine is empty as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil direction is right of vertical and loose so you could improve Recoil Direction or look to boost Stability with the barrel perk to help hit crits, or Handling if you can land your shots. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For melee builds, in the first trait perk column Proximity Power stacks up ×3 times to provide +18 to your Melee attribute per stack on kills when another enemy's within 15m - a total a +54 for a refreshable 10s on further kills, so you can invest stat points into other armour attributes. You can pair that with Swashbuckler in the second column to generate up to ×5 damage stacks on kills for max ×1.33 damage, with a melee kill giving 5 stacks, for a refreshable 5s. Aegis ranks the Elliptical Orbit origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist, which changes to Outer Orbit for a refreshable 3s after sprinting for 1.5s which significantly boosts Ammo Generation. There's also the very handy B-Tier Photoinhibition which is excellent in PvE, boosting shield, overshield and barrier damage, disorienting for 5s on a shield break, and AoE disorienting enemies in a 5m radius on a matching Void shield break. If you're interested, the name draws a parallel between the Vex's fatalistic timeline-weaving mechanics and the tragic hubris of the Kentarch 3 - a fireteam of Guardians who accepted Dark powers within the Black Garden, representing the dangerous folly of pursuing forbidden, absolute knowledge while actively accelerating toward a grim, predetermined fate. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd Melee-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller

For a general purpose roll for clearing tougher Minor adds, in the first trait perk column Collective Demolition synergises with grenade builds by providing grenade ability energy on hits after interacting with an elemental pickup - Firesprites and Void Breaches for 8s, Ionic Traces, Stasis Shards for 4s and Strand Tangles and Arc Elemental Orbs 4s each for pickup and explosion when thrown. Otherwise Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 3s of each other, delaying the need to reload and almost doubling mag size if you can keep hitting crits or Subsistence reloads 20% of the magazine on kills, again delaying the need to reload. In the second column, for damage, there's Paracausal Affinity which boosts damage by ×1.2 for a refreshable 6s after any kill of a Light energy type. Aegis ranks the Elliptical Orbit origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - it passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist, which changes to Outer Orbit for a refreshable 3s after sprinting for 1.5s which significantly boosts Ammo Generation. // The Ringing Nail - PvE Minors god AB 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Heal Clip), (Firefly, Onslaught, Rampage, Sword Logic)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty. The Ringing Nail is a precision auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "worse damage profile than balanced, but has barrier intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, and a ×0.9 reduction in horizontal recoil, as per the Destiny Data Compendium. With the frame intrinsic, you don't really have to worry about Recoil Direction, so you can take your pick with the barrel perk to boost Handling, Stability or Range. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For clearing tougher individual adds, in first trait perk column, Heal Clip provides ×2 Solar Cure (140 HP), and ×1 Cure on allies within 15m, when you finish a reload within ~5.5s after a kill. In the second column, Firefly procs a 4m AoE Solar explosion on a crit kill plus +50 Reload Speed for 6s - this also counts as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts, and also proc subclass fragments. Alternatively, Onslaught, while not boosting damage per shot does increase raw DPS as you ramp up to the max ×3 stacks, for a refreshable 5s per kill, to increase RPM from 450 to around max 720, Rampage stacks up to ×3 on kills for max ×1.331 damage for a refreshable 5s, with the stacks decaying one at a time, or there's also Sword Logic which provides up to ×4 stacks depending on the type of enemy killed - a ×1.15 boost for 6.5s on killing a Minor add, ×1.25 for 8.5s for an Elite, ×1.35 for 11.5s for a Champion or Miniboss, and ×1.5 for 16.5s for a Boss, with all the timers refreshed at their current tier on further kills. Aegis ranks the Forge's Kin origin trait as S-Tier for PvE - it provides a ×1 stack of "Forge's Kin" for 5s after a kill, or on swapping from another Black Armory weapon kill within 7s. Each additional Black Armory weapon equipped adds an additional stack, each boosting the activation window by +2s together with increasing Reload Speed and Ammo Generation, and applying damage boosts of ×1.1 for one or ×1.13 for two. If you're interested, the name is drawn from from the traditional art of blacksmithing and metallurgy. The phrase literally refers to the distinct, high-pitched acoustic resonance (the "ringing") produced when a blacksmith strikes a high-quality, hot metal nail or anvil with a hammer. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// The Ringing Nail - PvE Minors god AB 2
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Rewind Rounds), (Target Lock)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. The Ringing Nail is a precision auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "worse damage profile than balanced, but has barrier intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, and a ×0.9 reduction in horizontal recoil, as per the Destiny Data Compendium. With the frame intrinsic, you don't really have to worry about Recoil Direction, so you can take your pick with the barrel perk to boost Handling, Stability or Range. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For clearing tougher individual adds, in first trait perk column, Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the magazine empties. In the second column, Target Lock stacks up to 5 times on repeated hits within 0.2s of each other, for an average ×1.2813 damage once you've emptied your mag. Aegis ranks the Forge's Kin origin trait as S-Tier for PvE - it provides a ×1 stack of "Forge's Kin" for 5s after a kill, or on swapping from another Black Armory weapon kill within 7s. Each additional Black Armory weapon equipped adds an additional stack, each boosting the activation window by +2s together with increasing Reload Speed and Ammo Generation, and applying damage boosts of ×1.1 for one or ×1.13 for two. If you're interested, the name is drawn from from the traditional art of blacksmithing and metallurgy. The phrase literally refers to the distinct, high-pitched acoustic resonance (the "ringing") produced when a blacksmith strikes a high-quality, hot metal nail or anvil with a hammer. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds. |tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// The Ringing Nail - PvE Minors god AB 3
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell, Light Mag), (Rewind Rounds), (Onslaught, Rampage, Sword Logic)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty. The Ringing Nail is a precision auto rifle, which are rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "worse damage profile than balanced, but has barrier intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, and a ×0.9 reduction in horizontal recoil, as per the Destiny Data Compendium. With the frame intrinsic, you don't really have to worry about Recoil Direction, so you can take your pick with the barrel perk to boost Handling, Stability or Range. In the magazine perk slot, the combat effect perks work well, or look for perks that improve mag size or Reload Speed. For clearing tougher individual adds, in first trait perk column, Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the magazine empties. In the second column, Onslaught, while not boosting damage per shot does increase raw DPS as you ramp up to the max ×3 stacks, for a refreshable 5s per kill, to increase RPM from 450 to around max 720 - both works best with perks that delay the need to reload, Like Rewind Rounds Alternatively, Rampage stacks up to ×3 on kills for max ×1.331 damage for a refreshable 5s, with the stacks decaying one at a time, or there's also Sword Logic which provides up to ×4 stacks depending on the type of enemy killed - a ×1.15 boost for 6.5s on killing a Minor add, ×1.25 for 8.5s for an Elite, ×1.35 for 11.5s for a Champion or Miniboss, and ×1.5 for 16.5s for a Boss, with all the timers refreshed at their current tier on further kills. Aegis ranks the Forge's Kin origin trait as S-Tier for PvE - it provides a ×1 stack of "Forge's Kin" for 5s after a kill, or on swapping from another Black Armory weapon kill within 7s. Each additional Black Armory weapon equipped adds an additional stack, each boosting the activation window by +2s together with increasing Reload Speed and Ammo Generation, and applying damage boosts of ×1.1 for one or ×1.13 for two. If you're interested, the name is drawn from from the traditional art of blacksmithing and metallurgy. The phrase literally refers to the distinct, high-pitched acoustic resonance (the "ringing") produced when a blacksmith strikes a high-quality, hot metal nail or anvil with a hammer.
